# M3 建模功能逐项对照与能力计划

日期：2026-09-14。来源为本机 Maya 2026 官方菜单、运行时命令身份与默认键位文件，以及本机 Blender 源码。只整理功能身份和差异，没有复制 Autodesk 实现。

新增 562 个语义命令。Maya 去重动作分类：adapted 301；deferred 262；planned 197；dynamic_dependency 3。

M3 的交付边界是原生能力接入、默认键位核实、逐项映射和确实缺失能力的独立计划；不是 Maya 所有算法完全等价。完整绘制/动画/绑定及 UV 后置。

所有本轮人工验证状态仍为待测，集中见 `../compatibility/2026-09-14-manual-test-ledger.md`。下表包含菜单路径、作用模式、原生调用、默认输入、参数与差异；完整机器记录见同名 JSON。

## 已接入命令

| ID / 菜单 | 标签 / 类型 | 模式 / 原生入口 | 默认输入 / 参数 | 差异 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| selection.invert / Select > Selection | Invert / adapted | OBJECT / object.select_all EXEC; EDIT_MESH / mesh.select_all EXEC; EDIT_CURVE,EDIT_SURFACE / curve.select_all EXEC; EDIT_LATTICE / lattice.select_all EXEC | ctrl+shift+I / Edit > Adjust Last Operation 或原生属性 | Uses Blender native selection, geometry and parameter semantics. |
| selection.hierarchy / Select > Hierarchy | Hierarchy / adapted | OBJECT / axismeld.m3_select EXEC | 无AxisMeld专用绑定 / Edit > Adjust Last Operation 或原生属性 | Selects visible selectable Blender descendants; no Maya UFE/DAG nodes. |
| selection.geometry / Select > All by Type | All Geometry / adapted | OBJECT / axismeld.m3_select EXEC | 无AxisMeld专用绑定 / Edit > Adjust Last Operation 或原生属性 | Uses Blender native selection, geometry and parameter semantics. |
| selection.transforms / Select > All by Type | All Objects / adapted | OBJECT / object.select_all EXEC | 无AxisMeld专用绑定 / Edit > Adjust Last Operation 或原生属性 | Blender objects carry transforms; this excludes Maya transform-only DAG nodes. |
| selection.image_planes / Select > All by Type | Image Empties / adapted | OBJECT / axismeld.m3_select EXEC | 无AxisMeld专用绑定 / Edit > Adjust Last Operation 或原生属性 | Uses Blender native selection, geometry and parameter semantics. |

|---|---|---|---|
| M3-P-SELECT-DEFORMER-NODES.SelectAllClusters / Clusters | Maya cluster and wire influence/cluster-curve nodes are not Blender object types. Selecting all Curve objects would also select ordinary geometry and is not this action. | A persisted relationship between each adapted deformer and its influence/handle objects. | SelectAllClusters: demonstrate the named behavior on real scene geometry and context, cancellation and one undo where applicable; verify behavior rather than operator launch. |
| M3-P-SELECT-DEFORMER-NODES.SelectAllWires / Wires | Maya cluster and wire influence/cluster-curve nodes are not Blender object types. Selecting all Curve objects would also select ordinary geometry and is not this action. | A persisted relationship between each adapted deformer and its influence/handle objects. | SelectAllWires: demonstrate the named behavior on real scene geometry and context, cancellation and one undo where applicable; verify behavior rather than operator launch. |

This remaining Maya node-specific influence/falloff/rest/inner-surface relation is not represented by those native settings. | Explicit deformer-specific influence and rest/target relationship model. | SoftModTool: Verify the named relationship or deformation on a geometry fixture, retained targets, evaluated output and one undo; never count an empty modifier as success. |
| M3-P-INDEPENDENT-PIVOTS.MatchPivots / Match Pivots | Blender has one object origin. Maya independent rotate/scale pivots, custom pivot baking and selection handles cannot be represented by toggling that origin. | Separate pivot/handle storage, transform compensation and viewport gizmos. | MatchPivots: demonstrate the named behavior on real scene geometry and context, cancellation and one undo where applicable; verify behavior rather than operator launch. |
| M3-P-INDEPENDENT-PIVOTS.ZeroPivot / Zero Pivot | Blender has one object origin. | M3-P-INDEPENDENT-PIVOTS.BakeCustomPivot / Bake Pivot | Blender has one object origin. Maya independent rotate/scale pivots, custom pivot baking and selection handles cannot be represented by toggling that origin. | Separate pivot/handle storage, transform compensation and viewport gizmos. | BakeCustomPivot: demonstrate the named behavior on real scene geometry and context, cancellation and one undo where applicable; verify behavior rather than operator launch. |
| M3-P-POINT-PAIR-ALIGNMENT.Snap2PointsTo2Points / 2 Points to 2 Points | Native snapping and object alignment are connected, but Blender has no native paired-source/target point-picking context for this Maya rigid alignment workflow. | An owned multi-pick context, correspondence validation, rigid-transform solve and cancellation rollback. | Snap2PointsTo2Points: demonstrate the named behavior on real scene geometry and context, cancellation and one undo where applicable; verify behavior rather than operator launch. |
| M3-P-POINT-PAIR-ALIGNMENT.Snap3PointsTo3Points / 3 Points to 3 Points | Native snapping and object alignment are connected, but Blender has no native paired-source/target point-picking context for this Maya rigid alignment workflow. | An owned multi-pick context, correspondence validation, rigid-transform solve and cancellation rollback. | Snap3PointsTo3Points: demonstrate the named behavior on real scene geometry and context, cancellation and one undo where applicable; verify behavior rather than operator launch. |

| M3-P-DG-EVALUATION.EnableAll / Evaluate All | Maya dependency-node evaluation and Global Stitch nodes are not Blender modifier-visibility state. Toggling viewport modifiers would not preserve the named evaluation semantics. | A dependency-node correspondence model and a decision about disabled-node output persistence. | EnableAll: demonstrate the named behavior on real scene geometry and context, cancellation and one undo where applicable; verify behavior rather than operator launch. |
| M3-P-DG-EVALUATION.DisableAll / Ignore All | Maya dependency-node evaluation and Global Stitch nodes are not Blender modifier-visibility state. Toggling viewport modifiers would not preserve the named evaluation semantics. | A dependency-node correspondence model and a decision about disabled-node output persistence. | EnableGlobalStitch: demonstrate the named behavior on real scene geometry and context, cancellation and one undo where applicable; verify behavior rather than operator launch. |

| M3-P-MESH.PolygonBooleanSlice / Slice | Maya slices only the first mesh surface into separate shells at operand intersections. Native UNION/DIFFERENCE/INTERSECT are already connected; their capped results do not reproduce this surface-preserving output. | M3-P-MESH-01; first-operand face provenance, shell separation and operand preservation. | Intersecting/disjoint/tangent meshes retain the first surface, create the expected distinct shells, preserve operands and undo/redo atomically. |
| M3-P-MESH.PolygonBooleanHolePunch / Hole Punch | Maya removes the first surface inside the cutters and leaves an open mesh. Native Difference caps the cut, so relabeling it would be incorrect. | M3-P-MESH-01; distinguish cap faces from original surface and remove only result caps. | Overlapping cubes create the expected open boundary rather than a closed Difference solid; operand roles and one-step undo/redo verified. |
| M3-P-MESH.PolygonBooleanCutOut / Cut Out | Maya keeps only the first surface inside cutters, with open boundaries. Native Intersection creates a closed combined solid. | M3-P-MESH-01; preserve source-face provenance through intersections and omit cutter/cap faces. | Keep only the clipped first-input surface for overlapping/disjoint/tangent operands; no accidental cap or cutter faces; atomic undo. |
| M3-P-MESH.PolygonBooleanSplitEdges / Split Edges | Maya inserts intersection edges only into the first mesh while preserving its surface. Native Edit Mesh Intersect currently acts on both face sets; a source/operand isolation adapter is still required. | M3-P-MESH-01; object-to-edit intersection isolation and topology/data-layer transfer. | First mesh geometry is preserved except for intersection edges, other meshes unchanged; attribute layers and single undo checked. |
| M3-P-MESH.PolygonCopy / Copy Attributes | The Maya polygon clipboard stores/pastes mesh attributes. Blender object copybuffer copies entire objects; native Data Transfer already covers direct selected-source transfer but is not an attribute clipboard. | M3-P-MESH-02; bounded typed color/normal clipboard, element correspondence and lifetime; UV portion remains deferred. | Copy, paste and clear only declared attribute domains; reject incompatible topology; preserve geometry and unrelated layers; paste one undo. |
| M3-P-MESH.PolygonPaste / Paste Attributes | The Maya polygon clipboard stores/pastes mesh attributes. Blender object copybuffer copies entire objects; native Data Transfer already covers direct selected-source transfer but is not an attribute clipboard. | M3-P-MESH-02; bounded typed color/normal clipboard, element correspondence and lifetime; UV portion remains deferred. | Copy, paste and clear only declared attribute domains; reject incompatible topology; preserve geometry and unrelated layers; paste one undo. |
| M3-P-MESH.PolygonClearClipboard / Clear Clipboard | The Maya polygon clipboard stores/pastes mesh attributes. Blender object copybuffer copies entire objects; native Data Transfer already covers direct selected-source transfer but is not an attribute clipboard. | M3-P-MESH-02; bounded typed color/normal clipboard, element correspondence and lifetime; UV portion remains deferred. | Copy, paste and clear only declared attribute domains; reject incompatible topology; preserve geometry and unrelated layers; paste one undo. |

| M3-P-MESH.PolyAssignSubdivHole / Assign Invisible Faces | Blender face hide is a viewport selection/display state and does not implement persistent invisible faces in subdivision evaluation. | M3-P-MESH-03; subdivision-hole data/evaluation semantics. | Assigned faces remain editable in the base mesh while subdivision/render evaluation omits them; boundaries, normals, save/reopen and undo verified. |
| M3-P-EDITMESH.ProjectCurveOnMesh / Project Curve on Mesh | Knife Project is connected as a topology cut and Shrinkwrap as deformation. Neither creates the Maya projected-curve dependency output with its persistent source/target relationship. | M3-P-EDITMESH-01; curve projection output and dependency lifecycle. | Create/update the projected curve on mesh while preserving source curves and target topology; transform updates, tolerance, cancel and undo tested. |
| M3-P-TOOLS.EnterConnectTool / Connect | Native Connect Path/Pairs commands are connected. A continuous pick-next-component Connect tool with preview/cancel ownership is not supplied by these immediate commands. | M3-P-TOOLS-01; component picking/preview session built on native connect operations. | Pick multiple valid components without leaving the tool; reject unrelated meshes; preview, Escape/focus cancellation and one undo per confirmed connection. |
| M3-P-CONTEXT.CreatePolygonTool / Create Polygon | Poly Build append/quad construction is connected, but it does not supply the Maya click-many-points arbitrary polygon/finish contract used by the reserved creation entry. | Existing M2c-P01; arbitrary polygon construction, planarity/self-intersection checks and gesture ownership. | Create a polygon from a sequence of viewport points; Enter confirms, Esc cancels without geometry, one undo removes the result. |
| M3-P-TOOLS.MakeHoleTool / Make Hole | Maya selects a face then a second face to define its hole (MayaStrings:17486). Blender Knife/Inset/Delete are available individually but do not implement that two-face continuous tool contract. | M3-P-TOOLS-01; face-loop correspondence and valid hole topology. | Two face picks produce the specified hole, with invalid/noncoplanar input handling, visible preview, cancel and atomic undo. |
| M3-P-TOOLS.MergeVertexTool / Target Weld | Poly Build can transform picked components and native merge commands exist. No verified target-pick/highlight/merge-on-target session currently matches the dedicated Maya Target Weld contract. | M3-P-TOOLS-01; explicit source/target picking and transform/merge ownership; do not substitute Merge Last. | Drag a source onto a highlighted valid target; preserve target position and attributes; no merge on empty release/Esc/focus loss; one undo. |
